Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Fence Spawner")

local SpawnedFences = {}

Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
	Title = "Spawn Fence",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		if not player then return end
		local char = player.Character
		if not char then return end

		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then return end

		local success, objects = pcall(function()
			return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://104216557096252")
		end)

		if success and objects and objects[1] then
			local asset = objects[1]
			asset.Name = "Fence_GG_EVADE_Lol"

			if asset:IsA("Model") then
				if not asset.PrimaryPart then
					local pp = asset:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
					if pp then 
						asset.PrimaryPart = pp 
					end
				end
			end

			asset.Parent = workspace

			local cf = (root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, -8)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)

			if asset:IsA("Model") then
				if asset.PrimaryPart then
					asset:PivotTo(cf)
				end
			elseif asset:IsA("BasePart") then
				asset.CFrame = cf
			end

			for _, child in pairs(asset:GetDescendants()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") then
					child.Anchored = true
				end
			end

			if asset:IsA("Model") then
				local boxPart = Instance.new("Part")
				boxPart.Name = "InvisParts"
				boxPart.Massless = true
				boxPart.Transparency = 1
				boxPart.Anchored = true
				boxPart.CanCollide = false
				boxPart.Size = Vector3.new(10.4, 6.7, 1.2)
				boxPart.CFrame = (cf * CFrame.new(0, 1.1, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(-90), 0)
				boxPart.Parent = asset

				local boundingBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
				boundingBox.Name = "BoundingBox"
				boundingBox.Adornee = boxPart
				boundingBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 255, 255)
				boundingBox.LineThickness = 0
				boundingBox.Transparency = 1
				boundingBox.Parent = asset
			end

			table.insert(SpawnedFences, asset)

			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Success",
				Content = "Fence Success Fully Loaded!",
				Duration = 2
			})
		else
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Error",
				Content = "Failed to load model assets.",
				Duration = 2
			})
		end
	end
})

Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
	Title = "Delete Fence",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		local deleted = 0

		for _, v in pairs(SpawnedFences) do
			if v and v.Parent then
				v:Destroy()
				deleted = deleted + 1
			end
		end

		table.clear(SpawnedFences)

		Fluent:Notify({
			Title = "Success",
			Content = "Deleted " .. deleted .. " Fence",
			Duration = 2
		})
	end
})

Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Crosshair")

do
	local AdvancedConfig = {
		Enabled = false,
		SpinEnabled = false,
		UseCustomAsset = false,
		UseCustomSize = false,
		RotationSpeed = 5,
		Size = 30,
		DefaultSize = 30,
		AssetId = "7831350554",
		DefaultAsset = "7831350554"
	}

	local function updateLogoProperties()
		local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		local pgui = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
		local sg = pgui and pgui:FindFirstChild("GribabasCenter")
		if sg then
			local img = sg:FindFirstChild("RotatingLogo")
			if img then
				local targetSize = AdvancedConfig.UseCustomSize and AdvancedConfig.Size or AdvancedConfig.DefaultSize
				local targetAsset = AdvancedConfig.UseCustomAsset and AdvancedConfig.AssetId or AdvancedConfig.DefaultAsset

				img.Size = UDim2.new(0, targetSize, 0, targetSize)
				img.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. targetAsset:gsub("%D", "")

				if not AdvancedConfig.SpinEnabled then
					img.Rotation = 0
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function startCheat()
		local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		local pgui = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

		if pgui:FindFirstChild("GribabasCenter") then return end

		local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		sg.Name = "GribabasCenter"
		sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
		sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
		sg.Parent = pgui

		local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel", sg)
		img.Name = "RotatingLogo"
		img.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		img.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		img.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		updateLogoProperties()

		task.spawn(function()
			while img and img.Parent and AdvancedConfig.Enabled do
				if AdvancedConfig.SpinEnabled then
					img.Rotation = img.Rotation + AdvancedConfig.RotationSpeed
				else
					img.Rotation = 0
				end
				task.wait()
			end
			if sg.Parent then sg:Destroy() end
		end)
	end

	Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("MainLogoToggle", {
		Title = "Show Logo",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(Value)
			AdvancedConfig.Enabled = Value
			if Value then startCheat() else updateLogoProperties() end
		end
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("AssetToggle", {
		Title = "Use Custom ID",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(Value)
			AdvancedConfig.UseCustomAsset = Value
			updateLogoProperties()
		end
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddInput("AssetInput", {
		Title = "Custom Asset ID",
		Default = AdvancedConfig.AssetId,
		Numeric = true,
		Finished = true,
		Callback = function(Value)
			AdvancedConfig.AssetId = Value
			if AdvancedConfig.UseCustomAsset then updateLogoProperties() end
		end
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("SpinToggle", {
		Title = "Enable Spin",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(Value)
			AdvancedConfig.SpinEnabled = Value
			updateLogoProperties()
		end
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddSlider("SpinSlider", {
		Title = "Spin Speed",
		Default = 5,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 100,
		Rounding = 1,
		Callback = function(Value)
			AdvancedConfig.RotationSpeed = Value
		end
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("SizeToggle", {
		Title = "Enable Custom Size",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(Value)
			AdvancedConfig.UseCustomSize = Value
			updateLogoProperties()
		end
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddSlider("SizeSlider", {
		Title = "Custom Size",
		Default = 30,
		Min = 10,
		Max = 300,
		Rounding = 0,
		Callback = function(Value)
			AdvancedConfig.Size = Value
			if AdvancedConfig.UseCustomSize then updateLogoProperties() end
		end
	})

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			if AdvancedConfig.Enabled then
				local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
				local pgui = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
				if pgui and not pgui:FindFirstChild("GribabasCenter") then
					startCheat()
				end
			end
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)
end

local function ClearLighting()
	for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name ~= "MenuBlur" then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

do
	Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Custom Skybox")

	local SkyboxFaces = {
		{ prop = "SkyboxBk", label = "Slot 1 — Back" },
		{ prop = "SkyboxFt", label = "Slot 2 — Front" },
		{ prop = "SkyboxLf", label = "Slot 3 — Left" },
		{ prop = "SkyboxRt", label = "Slot 4 — Right" },
		{ prop = "SkyboxUp", label = "Slot 5 — Top" },
		{ prop = "SkyboxDn", label = "Slot 6 — Bottom" },
	}
	local SkyboxValues = {}
	for _, face in ipairs(SkyboxFaces) do
		SkyboxValues[face.prop] = ""
		Tabs.Extension:AddInput("SkyIn_" .. face.prop, {
			Title     = face.label,
			Placeholder = "https://... or rbxasset://...",
			Numeric   = false,
			Finished  = false,
			Callback  = function(Value) SkyboxValues[face.prop] = Value end,
		})
	end

	local function ApplyCustomSkybox()
		local LightingSvc = game:GetService("Lighting")
		for _, v in pairs(LightingSvc:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Sky") then v:Destroy() end
		end
		local Sky = Instance.new("Sky")
		Sky.Name = "Sky"
		Sky.CelestialBodiesShown = false
		Sky.StarCount = 0
		local applied, failed = 0, 0
		if not isfolder("customsky") then makefolder("customsky") end
		for _, face in ipairs(SkyboxFaces) do
			local val = SkyboxValues[face.prop] or ""
			if val ~= "" then
				local ok, err = pcall(function()
					if val:sub(1, 4) == "http" then
						local fname = "customsky/Sky_" .. face.prop .. ".png"
						writefile(fname, game:HttpGet(val, true))
						Sky[face.prop] = getcustomasset(fname)
					else
						Sky[face.prop] = val
					end
				end)
				if ok then
					applied = applied + 1
				else
					failed = failed + 1
					Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Skybox Error", Content = face.prop .. " failed: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 60), Duration = 5 })
				end
			end
		end
		if applied > 0 then
			Sky.Parent = LightingSvc
			Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Skybox", Content = "Applied " .. applied .. "/6 face(s)" .. (failed > 0 and " | " .. failed .. " failed" or ""), Duration = 3 })
		else
			Sky:Destroy()
			Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Skybox", Content = "All faces failed. Check your URLs.", Duration = 5 })
		end
	end

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
		Title     = "Apply Custom Skybox",
		Description = "Supports https:// and rbxasset://",
		Callback  = function() task.spawn(ApplyCustomSkybox) end,
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Skybox")

	local function _makeSkybox(folder, faces)
		local LightingSvc = game:GetService("Lighting")
		for _, v in pairs(LightingSvc:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Sky") then v:Destroy() end
		end
		if not isfolder(folder) then makefolder(folder) end
		local Sky = Instance.new("Sky")
		Sky.Name = "Sky"
		Sky.CelestialBodiesShown = false
		Sky.StarCount = 0
		local loaded = 0
		for _, v in ipairs(faces) do
			local path = folder .. "/" .. v.file
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				if not isfile(path) then
					writefile(path, game:HttpGet(v.url, true))
				end
				Sky[v.prop] = getcustomasset(path)
			end)
			if ok then
				loaded = loaded + 1
			else
				pcall(function() if isfile(path) then delfile(path) end end)
				Fluent:Notify({
					Title = "Skybox Error",
					Content = v.file .. " failed: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 60),
					Duration = 5
				})
			end
		end
		if loaded > 0 then
			Sky.Parent = LightingSvc
			if loaded < #faces then
				Fluent:Notify({
					Title = "Skybox",
					Content = loaded .. "/" .. #faces .. " faces loaded",
					Duration = 4
				})
			end
		else
			Sky:Destroy()
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Skybox",
				Content = "Failed to load all faces. Try again.",
				Duration = 5
			})
		end
	end

	local _skyAutoApply = false
	local SkyboxDropdown

	local function _applySelectedSkybox()
		local selected = SkyboxDropdown and SkyboxDropdown.Value
		local fn = selected and BuiltinSkyboxes[selected]
		if fn then
			fn()
		else
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Skybox",
				Content = "Select a skybox from the dropdown first.",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end
	end

	SkyboxDropdown = Tabs.Extension:AddDropdown("BuiltinSkyboxDrop", {
		Title = "Built-in Skybox",
		Values= { "Hakari Hananozo",  "Nakano Miku", "Rias Gremory", "Xenovia Quarta", "Yotsuba Nakano", },
		Multi = false,
		Search= false,
		Default = 1,
	})
	SkyboxDropdown:OnChanged(function()
		if _skyAutoApply then _applySelectedSkybox() end
	end)

	Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("SkyAutoApplyToggle", {
		Title     = "Auto Apply",
		Description = "Automatically applies skybox when the dropdown changes",
		Default   = false,
		Callback  = function(Value)
			_skyAutoApply = Value
			if Value then _applySelectedSkybox() end
		end,
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
		Title     = "Apply Built-in Skybox",
		Description = "",
		Callback  = function() task.spawn(_applySelectedSkybox) end,
	})
end

do
	Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Effects Remover")

	local function _getLighting()
		return game:GetService("Lighting")
	end

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
		Title     = "Clear All Lighting Effects",
		Description = "Removes Fog, Atmosphere, Bloom, Blur, DepthOfField, SunRays, ColorCorrection",
		Callback  = function()
			local L = _getLighting()
			local removed = 0
			for _, v in pairs(L:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
					or v:IsA("BloomEffect")  or v:IsA("BlurEffect")
					or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") then
					v.Enabled = false
					removed = removed + 1
				end
			end
			L.FogEnd = 100000
			L.FogStart = 0
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Lighting",
				Content = "Removed " .. removed .. " effect(s) and reset fog.",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end,
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
		Title     = "Remove Fog",
		Description = "Pushes FogEnd to maximum to make fog invisible",
		Callback  = function()
			local L = _getLighting()
			L.FogEnd = 100000
			L.FogStart = 0
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Lighting",
				Content = "Fog removed.",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end,
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
		Title     = "Remove Atmosphere",
		Description = "Destroys the Atmosphere object from Lighting",
		Callback  = function()
			local L = _getLighting()
			local found = false
			for _, v in pairs(L:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Atmosphere") then v:Destroy(); found = true end
			end
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Lighting",
				Content = found and "Atmosphere removed." or "No Atmosphere found.",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end,
	})

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
		Title     = "Reset Lighting",
		Description = "Restores default Roblox lighting values",
		Callback  = function()
			local L = _getLighting()
			L.FogEnd = 100000
			L.FogStart = 0
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Lighting",
				Content = "Lighting reset to defaults.",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end,
	})
end

Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Lightning Extension")

local normalLighting = {
	Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
	ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,
	ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
	FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
	FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
	GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
	ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
	Brightness = Lighting.Brightness
}

local function applyFullBright()
	Lighting.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
	Lighting.FogStart = 0
	Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
	Lighting.ClockTime = 14
	Lighting.Brightness = 2
end

local function restoreLighting()
	for prop, value in pairs(normalLighting) do
		Lighting[prop] = value
	end
end

local Fullbrighting = false

local Toggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("FullBright", {Title = "Full Bright", Default = false})

Toggle:OnChanged(
	function(state)
		Fullbrighting = state
		if Fullbrighting then
			applyFullBright()
		else
			restoreLighting()
		end
	end
)

Options.FullBright:SetValue(false)

Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Render")

do
	local renderLoop = false
	local renderBusy = false
	local requestedCells = {}

	local CELL = 300      -- studs per streaming cell
	local PER_BATCH = 4   -- requests per step (keeps frame time flat)

	-- Collect one sample point per grid cell (cheap, done once, in slices)
	local function collectCells()
		local cells, seen, n = {}, {}, 0
		for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			n += 1
			if n % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
			if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Anchored then
				local p = obj.Position
				local key = math.floor(p.X / CELL) .. "," .. math.floor(p.Y / CELL) .. "," .. math.floor(p.Z / CELL)
				if not seen[key] then
					seen[key] = true
					cells[#cells + 1] = { key = key, pos = p }
				end
			end
		end
		return cells
	end

	local function renderWholeGame()
		if renderBusy then return 0 end
		renderBusy = true
		pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(Workspace, "StreamingMinRadius", 2048) end)
		pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(Workspace, "StreamingTargetRadius", 4096) end)

		local done = 0
		local ok = pcall(function()
			local cells = collectCells()
			for i, c in ipairs(cells) do
				if not requestedCells[c.key] then
					requestedCells[c.key] = true
					task.spawn(function()
						pcall(function() LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(c.pos, 0.05) end)
					end)
					done += 1
					if done % PER_BATCH == 0 then
						-- wait a few frames between batches so FPS stays stable
						for _ = 1, 3 do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		renderBusy = false
		return done
	end

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
		Title = "Render Entire Game",
		Description = "Streams the whole map in small batches (no FPS drop).",
		Callback = function()
			task.spawn(function()
				requestedCells = {}
				local n = renderWholeGame()
				Fluent:Notify({
					Title = "Render",
					Content = "Entire game rendered (" .. tostring(n) .. " areas loaded).",
					Duration = 3
				})
			end)
		end
	})

	local Toggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("Render_Entire_Game", {Title = "Auto Render Entire Game", Default = false})

	Toggle:OnChanged(function(Value)
		renderLoop = Value
		if Value then
			task.spawn(function()
				while renderLoop do
					renderWholeGame()
					task.wait(20)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

	Options.Render_Entire_Game:SetValue(false)

	-- Max performance: uncapped FPS + lowest-cost render settings
	local perfOn = false
	local perfBackup = {}

	local Perf = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("Max_Performance", {
		Title = "Max Performance (CPU/GPU)",
		Description = "Uncaps FPS and removes throttling so the device runs at full speed.",
		Default = false
	})

	Perf:OnChanged(function(Value)
		perfOn = Value
		if Value then
			pcall(function() perfBackup.fps = getfpscap and getfpscap() end)
			pcall(function() if setfpscap then setfpscap(0) end end)
			pcall(function() perfBackup.q = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel end)
			pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end)
			pcall(function() perfBackup.ms = settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel end)
			pcall(function() settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level04 end)
			pcall(function() perfBackup.sh = Lighting.GlobalShadows end)
			pcall(function() Lighting.GlobalShadows = false end)
			pcall(function() settings().Physics.AllowSleep = false end)
			pcall(function() settings().Network.IncomingReplicationLag = 0 end)
			pcall(function() RunService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(true) end)
			Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Performance", Content = "Max performance mode ON", Duration = 3 })
		else
			pcall(function() if setfpscap then setfpscap(perfBackup.fps or 60) end end)
			pcall(function() if perfBackup.q then settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = perfBackup.q end end)
			pcall(function() if perfBackup.ms then settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = perfBackup.ms end end)
			pcall(function() if perfBackup.sh ~= nil then Lighting.GlobalShadows = perfBackup.sh end end)
			Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Performance", Content = "Max performance mode OFF", Duration = 3 })
		end
	end)

	Options.Max_Performance:SetValue(false)
end

Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Anti Lags Extension")

local Lag1 = false

local Toggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("Anti_Lag1", {Title = "Anti Lag 1", Default = false})

Toggle:OnChanged(
	function(Value1)
		Lag1 = Value1
		if Lag1 then
			for _, v in pairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if v:IsA("BasePart") and not v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					v.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
					if v:IsA("Texture") then
						v:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
)

Options.Anti_Lag1:SetValue(false)

local Toggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("Anti_Lag2", {Title = "Anti Lag 2", Default = false})

Toggle:OnChanged(
	function(Value3)
		if Value3 then
			local decalsyeeted = true -- Leaving this on makes games look shitty but the fps goes up by at least 20.
			local g = game
			local w = g.Workspace
			local l = g.Lighting
			local t = w.Terrain
			t.WaterWaveSize = 0
			t.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
			t.WaterReflectance = 0
			t.WaterTransparency = 0
			l.GlobalShadows = false
			l.FogEnd = 9e9
			l.Brightness = 0
			settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"
			wait(1)
			for i, v in pairs(g:GetDescendants()) do
				if v:IsA("Part") or v:IsA("Union") or v:IsA("MeshPart") then
					v.Material = "Plastic"
					v.Reflectance = 0
				elseif v:IsA("Decal") and decalsyeeted then
					v.Transparency = 1
				elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") then
					v.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
				end
			end
		end
	end
)

Options.Anti_Lag2:SetValue(false)

local Toggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("Anti_Lag3", {Title = "Anti Lag 3", Default = false})

Toggle:OnChanged(
	function(Value4)
		if Value4 then
			local decalsyeeted = true
			local g = game
			local w = g.Workspace
			local l = g.Lighting
			local t = w.Terrain
			sethiddenproperty(l, "Technology", 2)
			sethiddenproperty(t, "Decoration", false)
			t.WaterWaveSize = 0
			t.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
			t.WaterReflectance = 0
			t.WaterTransparency = 0
			l.GlobalShadows = 0
			l.FogEnd = 9e9
			l.Brightness = 0
			settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"
			for i, v in pairs(w:GetDescendants()) do
				if v:IsA("BasePart") and not v:IsA("MeshPart") then
					v.Material = "Plastic"
					v.Reflectance = 0
				elseif (v:IsA("Decal") or v:IsA("Texture")) and decalsyeeted then
					v.Transparency = 1
				elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") then
					v.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
				elseif v:IsA("Explosion") then
					v.BlastPressure = 1
					v.BlastRadius = 1
				elseif v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("SpotLight") or v:IsA("Smoke") or v:IsA("Sparkles") then
					v.Enabled = false
				elseif v:IsA("MeshPart") and decalsyeeted then
					v.Material = "Plastic"
					v.Reflectance = 0
					v.TextureID = 10385902758728957
				elseif v:IsA("SpecialMesh") and decalsyeeted then
					v.TextureId = 0
				elseif v:IsA("ShirtGraphic") and decalsyeeted then
					v.Graphic = 0
				elseif (v:IsA("Shirt") or v:IsA("Pants")) and decalsyeeted then
					v[v.ClassName .. "Template"] = 0
				end
			end
			for i = 1, #l:GetChildren() do
				e = l:GetChildren()[i]
				if
					e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or
					e:IsA("BloomEffect") or
					e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect")
				then
					e.Enabled = false
				end
			end
			w.DescendantAdded:Connect(
				function(v)
					task.wait(1)
					if v:IsA("BasePart") and not v:IsA("MeshPart") then
						v.Material = "Plastic"
						v.Reflectance = 0
					elseif v:IsA("Decal") or v:IsA("Texture") and decalsyeeted then
						v.Transparency = 1
					elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") then
						v.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
					elseif v:IsA("Explosion") then
						v.BlastPressure = 1
						v.BlastRadius = 1
					elseif v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("SpotLight") or v:IsA("Smoke") or v:IsA("Sparkles") then
						v.Enabled = false
					elseif v:IsA("MeshPart") and decalsyeeted then
						v.Material = "Plastic"
						v.Reflectance = 0
						v.TextureID = 10385902758728957
					elseif v:IsA("SpecialMesh") and decalsyeeted then
						v.TextureId = 0
					elseif v:IsA("ShirtGraphic") and decalsyeeted then
						v.ShirtGraphic = 0
					elseif (v:IsA("Shirt") or v:IsA("Pants")) and decalsyeeted then
						v[v.ClassName .. "Template"] = 0
					end
				end
			)
		end
	end
)

Options.Anti_Lag3:SetValue(false)

Tabs.Extension:AddButton({
	Title = "No Render",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
		local Terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
		local Players = game:GetService("Players")
		Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
		Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10
		Lighting.Brightness = 1
		if Terrain then
			Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
			Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
			Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
			Terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
		end
		for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
				obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
				obj.Reflectance = 0
			elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") or obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") then
				obj:Destroy()
			elseif obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
				obj:Destroy()
			end
		end
		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local char = player.Character
			if char then
				for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
					if part:IsA("Accessory") or part:IsA("Clothing") then
						part:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
})

Tabs.Extension:AddParagraph({
	Title = " ",
	Content = ""
})

local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local defaultGlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
local defaultTechnology = Lighting.Technology

local ShadowsToggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("ShadowsToggle", {
	Title = "Remove All Shadows",
	Description = "",
	Default = false
})

ShadowsToggle:OnChanged(function(state)
	pcall(function()
		Lighting.GlobalShadows = not state
		Lighting.Technology = state and Enum.Technology.Compatibility or defaultTechnology

		task.spawn(function()
			for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
					obj.CastShadow = not state
				end
			end
		end)
	end)
end)

local DarknessToggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("DarknessToggle", {
	Title = "Disable Light",
	Description = "",
	Default = false
})

DarknessToggle:OnChanged(function(state)
	task.spawn(function()
		for _, light in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if light:IsA("Light") then
				pcall(function()
					light.Enabled = not state
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end)

getgenv().FpsConfig = {
	Enabled = false
}

local function updateFps()
	pcall(function()
		local target = getgenv().FpsConfig.Enabled and 9999 or 60
		if setfflag then
			setfflag("TaskSchedulerTargetFps", tostring(target))
			setfflag("DFIntTaskSchedulerTargetFps", tostring(target))
		end
		if setfpscap then
			setfpscap(target)
		end
	end)
end

local networkPausedConn = nil
local AntiGPTPause = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("AntiNetworkPause", {
	Title = "Anti Gameplay Paused", 
	Default = false, 
	Description = ""
})

AntiGPTPause:OnChanged(function(Value)
	if Value then
		pcall(function()
			local RobloxGui = game:GetService("CoreGui"):WaitForChild("RobloxGui")
			local currentPause = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("CoreScripts/NetworkPause")

			if currentPause then 
				currentPause:Destroy() 
			end

			networkPausedConn = RobloxGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
				if obj.Name == "CoreScripts/NetworkPause" then
					task.wait() 
					obj:Destroy()
				end
			end)
		end)
	else
		if networkPausedConn then
			networkPausedConn:Disconnect()
			networkPausedConn = nil
		end
	end
end)

local FpsUnlockToggle = Tabs.Extension:AddToggle("FpsUnlockToggle", {
	Title = "Unlock FPS",
	Description = "",
	Default = false
})

FpsUnlockToggle:OnChanged(function(Value)
	getgenv().FpsConfig.Enabled = Value
	updateFps()
end)

game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
	pcall(function()
		character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
		task.wait(1)
		if getgenv().FpsConfig.Enabled then
			updateFps()
		end
	end)
end)

if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character and getgenv().FpsConfig.Enabled then
	updateFps()
end

Tabs.Extension:AddSection("Fast Flag Extension")

if setfflag then
	Tabs.Extension:AddButton(
		{
			Title = "Blox Strap Script",
			Description = "",
			Callback = function()
				loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/qwertyui-is-back/Bloxstrap/main/Initiate.lua'), 'lol')()
			end
		}
	)

	Tabs.Extension:AddButton(
		{
			Title = "Accurate Low Quality",
			Description = "(FastFlag)",
			Callback = function()

				if setfpscap then
					pcall(function() setfpscap(900) end)
				end

				local function safeSetFFlag(flag, value)
					pcall(function()
						if setfflag then
							setfflag(flag, value)
						end
					end)
				end

				safeSetFFlag("TaskSchedulerTargetFps", "900")

				local function removeWater()
					for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
						if obj:IsA("Terrain") then
							obj.WaterTransparency = 1
							obj.WaterWaveSize = 0
							obj.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
							obj.WaterReflectance = 0
						end
					end
				end

				local function removeReflections()
					local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
					lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
					lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
				end

				local function removeEffects()
					local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
					for _, effect in pairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
						if effect:IsA("BlurEffect") or effect:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or effect:IsA("BloomEffect") or effect:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
							effect.Enabled = false
						end
					end

					for _, effect in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
						if
							effect:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or effect:IsA("Smoke") or effect:IsA("Fire") or
							effect:IsA("Sparkles")
						then
							effect.Enabled = false
						end
					end
				end

				local function removeGrass()
					safeSetFFlag("FRMMinGrassDistance", "0")
					safeSetFFlag("FRMMaxGrassDistance", "0")
					safeSetFFlag("RenderGrassDetailStrands", "0")
				end

				local function removeExplosions()
					for _, explosion in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
						if explosion:IsA("Explosion") then
							explosion:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end

				local function setLowShadows()
					pcall(function()
						game.Lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Voxel
					end)
					game.Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
				end

				local function setLowQuality()
					pcall(function()
						settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
					end)
				end

				local function setFlagsGraphics()
					safeSetFFlag("DebugGraphicsPreferVulkan", "true")
					safeSetFFlag("DebugTextureManagerSkipMips", "2")
					safeSetFFlag("TaskSchedulerLimitTargetFpsTo2402", "false")
					safeSetFFlag("TaskSchedulerTargetFps", "900")   
					safeSetFFlag("FFlagDebugDisableDirectMotionBlur", "true")
					safeSetFFlag("FFlagGraphicsGnm", "true") 
					safeSetFFlag("DFFlagTextureQualityOverride", "1")
					safeSetFFlag("FFlagRenderLocalLightsFrontToBack", "true")
					safeSetFFlag("FFlagRenderFixAA", "false")
					safeSetFFlag("DFreqRenderAntialiasing", "0")
					safeSetFFlag("FFlagVisualEngineEnableLOD", "true")
					safeSetFFlag("FFlagDebugForceAllDecalsLowRes", "true")
				end

				local function reduceLag()
					removeWater()
					removeReflections()
					removeEffects()
					removeExplosions()
					setLowShadows()
					setLowQuality()
					setFlagsGraphics()
					removeGrass()
				end

				reduceLag()

				workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(
					function(descendant)
						if descendant:IsA("Terrain") then
							task.wait(0.5)
							reduceLag()
						end
					end
				)
			end
		}
	)
end

local oldNamecall
oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(function(self, ...)
	local Args = {...}
	local method = getnamecallmethod()

	if self.Name == "Communicator" and method == "InvokeServer" and Args[1] == "update" then
		local SpeedCap = DConfiguration.Misc.PlayerAdjustment.Update.RealSpeed or 1500
		local JumpHeight = DConfiguration.Misc.PlayerAdjustment.Update.RealJumpHeight or 3

		return SpeedCap, JumpHeight
	end

	return oldNamecall(self, ...)
end))

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
	task.delay(5, function()
		DFunctions.HookMovement(character)
	end)
end)

if LocalPlayer.Character then
	DFunctions.HookMovement(LocalPlayer.Character)
end

-- Build name list for dropdown
local overhaulEmoteNames = {}
for _, e in ipairs(EMOTE_LIST) do table.insert(overhaulEmoteNames, e.Name) end

-- State (one table = one local, keeps us under Lua's 200-local cap)
local OE = {
	selectedName = EMOTE_LIST[1].Name,
	selectedID   = EMOTE_LIST[1].ID,
	isActive     = false,
	enabled      = false,
	keybind      = "E",
	character    = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait(),
	animTrack    = nil,
	blockConn    = nil,
	lookYConn    = nil,
	mobileGui    = nil,
}


-- Helper: stop anim
local function OE_stopAnim()
	if OE.animTrack then OE.animTrack:Stop(); OE.animTrack = nil end
end

-- Helper: loop the selected animation
local function OE_animLoop()
	while OE.character and OE.character.Parent and OE.isActive do
		local hum = OE.character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if not hum then break end
		local anim8r = hum:FindFirstChild("Animator")
		if not anim8r then break end
		for _, t in pairs(anim8r:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
			if t.Name == "OE_ForceAnim" then t:Stop() end
		end
		local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
		anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. OE.selectedID
		anim.Name = "OE_ForceAnim"
		OE.animTrack = anim8r:LoadAnimation(anim)
		OE.animTrack.Looped = true
		OE.animTrack:Play()
		while OE.character and OE.character.Parent and OE.isActive
			and OE.animTrack and OE.animTrack.IsPlaying do
			task.wait(0.1)
		end
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
	OE_stopAnim()
end

-- Helper: create EmoteSpeed value so the game thinks we're emoting
local function OE_createEmoteSpeed()
	local me = workspace.Game.Players:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
	if not me then return false end
	local sc = me:FindFirstChild("StatChanges")
	if not sc then sc = Instance.new("Folder"); sc.Name = "StatChanges"; sc.Parent = me end
	local sp = sc:FindFirstChild("Speed")
	if not sp then sp = Instance.new("Folder"); sp.Name = "Speed"; sp.Parent = sc end
	local es = sp:FindFirstChild("EmoteSpeed")
	if not es then
		es = Instance.new("NumberValue"); es.Name = "EmoteSpeed"; es.Value = 1; es.Parent = sp
	else
		es.Value = 1
	end
	return true
end

local function OE_removeEmoteSpeed()
	local me = workspace.Game.Players:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
	if not me then return end
	local sc = me:FindFirstChild("StatChanges")
	if not sc then return end
	local sp = sc:FindFirstChild("Speed")
	if not sp then return end
	local es = sp:FindFirstChild("EmoteSpeed")
	if es then es:Destroy() end
end

-- Helper: block Crouch/Slide animations while active
local function OE_startAnimBlock()
	if not OE.character then return end
	local hum = OE.character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	if not hum then return end
	local anim8r = hum:FindFirstChild("Animator")
	if not anim8r then return end
	local bad = {"Crouch","Slide"}
	local function check(track)
		if not (track and track.Stop) then return end
		local a = track.Animation
		if not a then return end
		local n, id = a.Name or "", a.AnimationId or ""
		for _, w in pairs(bad) do
			if string.find(n, w) or string.find(id, w) then track:Stop(); pcall(function() track:Destroy() end); break end
		end
	end
	for _, t in pairs(anim8r:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do check(t) end
	if OE.blockConn then OE.blockConn:Disconnect() end
	OE.blockConn = anim8r.AnimationPlayed:Connect(check)
end

local function OE_stopAnimBlock()
	if OE.blockConn then OE.blockConn:Disconnect(); OE.blockConn = nil end
end

-- Helper: force LookY=0 so camera doesn't tilt
local function OE_startLookYForce()
	if OE.lookYConn then OE.lookYConn:Disconnect() end
	OE.lookYConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not OE.isActive then return end
		pcall(function()
			if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("LookY") ~= nil then LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("LookY", 0) end
			if LocalPlayer.Character then LocalPlayer.Character:SetAttribute("LookY", 0) end
		end)
	end)
end

local function OE_stopLookYForce()
	if OE.lookYConn then OE.lookYConn:Disconnect(); OE.lookYConn = nil end
end

-- Camera / ShiftLock helpers
local function OE_cameraOn()
	local p = LocalPlayer
	p.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 10
	p.CameraMinZoomDistance = 10
	p.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic
	local ps = p:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
	if ps then
		local mlo = ps:FindFirstChild("MouseLockOffset")
		if mlo then mlo.Value = Vector3.new(0,0,0) end
		local sle = ps:FindFirstChild("ShiftLockEnabled")
		if sle and sle:IsA("BoolValue") then sle.Value = true end
	end
	local wp = workspace.Game.Players:FindFirstChild(p.Name)
	if wp then wp:SetAttribute("FirstPerson", false) end
end

local function OE_cameraOff()
	local p = LocalPlayer
	p.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 0.502
	p.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
	p.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson
	local ps = p:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
	if ps then
		local sle = ps:FindFirstChild("ShiftLockEnabled")
		if sle and sle:IsA("BoolValue") then sle.Value = false end
	end
	local wp = workspace.Game.Players:FindFirstChild(p.Name)
	if wp then wp:SetAttribute("FirstPerson", true) end
end

-- Master on / off
local function OE_activate()
	if OE.isActive then return end
	OE.isActive = true
	task.spawn(OE_animLoop)
	task.spawn(function()
		local tries = 0
		while OE.isActive and tries < 20 do
			if OE_createEmoteSpeed() then break end
			tries += 1; task.wait(1)
		end
	end)
	OE_startAnimBlock()
	OE_startLookYForce()
	OE_cameraOn()
	Fluent:Notify({ Title="Overhaul Emote", Content=OE.selectedName.." on!", Duration=3 })
end

local function OE_deactivate()
	if not OE.isActive then return end
	OE.isActive = false
	OE_stopAnim()
	OE_removeEmoteSpeed()
	OE_stopAnimBlock()
	OE_stopLookYForce()
	OE_cameraOff()
	Fluent:Notify({ Title="Overhaul Emote", Content=OE.selectedName.." stopped.", Duration=2 })
end

-- Master gate: toggle controls whether keybind/button work at all

-- Emote play/stop via keybind or mobile button
local function OE_toggle()
	if not OE.enabled then return end  -- gate: do nothing if master switch is off
	if OE.isActive then OE_deactivate() else OE_activate() end
end

-- Character respawn handling
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
	OE.character = newChar
	if OE.isActive then
		OE_stopAnim()
		OE_stopAnimBlock()
		task.wait(0.5)
		if OE.isActive then
			task.spawn(OE_animLoop)
			OE_startAnimBlock()
		end
	end
end)

-- Mobile button (created once, shown/hidden by the master toggle)
local function OE_isMobile()
	return UserInputService.TouchEnabled
		and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
		and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled
end

if OE_isMobile() then
	task.spawn(function()
		local pg = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
		if pg:FindFirstChild("OE_ColaGui") then pg.OE_ColaGui:Destroy() end

		local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		gui.Name = "OE_ColaGui"; gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		gui.Enabled = false  -- hidden until master toggle is ON
		gui.Parent = pg
		OE.mobileGui = gui

		local sc = Instance.new("UIScale")
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local function upd() sc.Scale = math.clamp(cam.ViewportSize.Y/720, 0.85, 1.35) end
		upd(); cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(upd); sc.Parent = gui

		local btn = Instance.new("ImageButton", gui)
		btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		btn.Size = UDim2.new(0,80,0,80)
		btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,-40,0.5,-40)
		btn.Image = "rbxassetid://16803802267"

		local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos
		btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
			if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging=true; dragInput=i; dragStart=i.Position; startPos=btn.Position
			end
		end)
		btn.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
			if i==dragInput and dragging then
				local d=i.Position-dragStart
				btn.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+d.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+d.Y)
			end
		end)
		UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
			if i==dragInput then dragging=false; dragInput=nil end
		end)

		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(OE_toggle)
	end)
end

-- PC keybind — gated by OE.enabled
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
	if gp or not OE.enabled then return end
	local ok, key = pcall(function()
		local s = OE.keybind:upper()
		return s == "SPACEBAR" and Enum.KeyCode.Space or Enum.KeyCode[s]
	end)
	if ok and key and input.KeyCode == key then OE_toggle() end
end)

-- === EMOTES TAB UI ===
local secOE = Tabs.Emotes:AddSection("Overhaul Emotes")

Tabs.Emotes:AddParagraph({
	Title = "Overhaul Emotes",
	Content = "Toggle ON to activate the keybind (PC) or show the button (Mobile). Use the keybind/button to start and stop the emote.",
})

-- Master toggle — gates keybind and mobile button visibility
Tabs.Emotes:AddToggle("OE_Toggle", {
	Title       = "Enable Overhaul Emote System",
	Description = "ON = keybind active / mobile button visible. OFF = both disabled.",
	Icon        = "music",
	Default     = false,
	Callback    = function(v)
		OE.enabled = v
		-- show or hide the mobile button
		if OE.mobileGui then OE.mobileGui.Enabled = v end
		-- if turning off while emote is playing, stop it
		if not v and OE.isActive then OE_deactivate() end
	end,
})

-- Emote dropdown
Tabs.Emotes:AddDropdown("OE_Dropdown", {
	Title       = "Select Emote",
	Description = "Choose from 150+ overhaul emotes",
	Icon        = "list",
	Values      = overhaulEmoteNames,
	Default     = overhaulEmoteNames[1],
	Callback    = function(v)
		OE.selectedName = v
		for _, e in ipairs(EMOTE_LIST) do
			if e.Name == v then OE.selectedID = e.ID; break end
		end
		-- if already running, restart with new emote
		if OE.isActive then
			OE_stopAnim()
			task.wait(0.15)
			task.spawn(OE_animLoop)
			Fluent:Notify({ Title="Emote Switched", Content="Now playing: "..v, Duration=2 })
		end
	end,
})

-- Keybind input
Tabs.Emotes:AddInput("OE_Keybind", {
	Title       = "PC Keybind",
	Description = "Key to toggle emote on/off",
	Icon        = "keyboard",
	Placeholder = "E",
	Default     = "E",
	Callback    = function(v)
		if v and v ~= "" then
			OE.keybind = v:gsub("^%s*",""):gsub("%s*$",""):upper()
			Fluent:Notify({ Title="Keybind", Content="Set to: "..OE.keybind, Duration=2 })
		end
	end,
})

})

Tabs.Settings:AddButton({
	Title = "Having trouble with interface?",
	Description = "Click this button to destroy the interface.",
	Icon = "shield-alert",
	Callback = function()
		Window:Dialog({
			Title = "Close", 
			Content = "Are you sure you want to unload the interface?",
			Buttons = {
				{
					Title = "Yes",
					Callback = function()
						if Fluent and Fluent.Unload then
							pcall(function() Fluent:Unload() end)
						elseif Fluent and Fluent.GUI then
							pcall(function() Fluent.GUI:Destroy() end)
						end

						task.spawn(function()
							local players = game:GetService("Players")
							local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
							local playerGui = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

							while task.wait(0.5) do
								if Fluent.Unloaded == true or (Fluent.GUI and Fluent.GUI.Parent == nil) then
									openGui:Destroy()
									if fpsCounterGui then fpsCounterGui:Destroy() end

									_G.HyperionHubXIsAlreadyRunning = nil
									break
								end
							end
						end)
					end
				},
				{
					Title = "No",
					Callback = function()
					end
				},
			},
		})
	end
})

	Title = "Performance stats",
	Callback = function(Value)

			CreateFpsCounter()
		else
			DestroyFpsCounter()
		end
	end
})

-- === OLD FOLDER: legacy boost tools (from hi.txt) ===
do
	local tab = Tabs.OldFolder
	if tab then
		tab:AddParagraph({
			Title = "Legacy Boost Tools",
			Content = "Cola Boost, Speed Pad Boost and Player Speed overrides. Values stay active while the script is running.",
		})

		local colaBoostValue = 1.4
		local speedPadValue = 1.3

		local function monitorBoostObject(object)
			if not object or not object:IsA("NumberValue") then return end
			if object.Name == "ColaBoost" then
				pcall(function() object.Value = colaBoostValue end)
				object:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
					if object.Value ~= colaBoostValue then
						pcall(function() object.Value = colaBoostValue end)
					end
				end)
			elseif object.Name == "SpeedPad" then
				pcall(function() object.Value = speedPadValue end)
				object:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
					if object.Value ~= speedPadValue then
						pcall(function() object.Value = speedPadValue end)
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		local function checkDescendant(desc)
			local parent = desc and desc.Parent
			if parent and parent.Name == "Speed" then
				monitorBoostObject(desc)
			end
		end

		pcall(function()
			for _, item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				checkDescendant(item)
			end
			Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(checkDescendant)
		end)

		tab:AddSection("Speed Changer (Boost)")

		tab:AddInput("OldFolder_ColaBoost", {
			Title = "Cola Boost Changer",
			Default = "1.4",
			Placeholder = "1.4",
			Callback = function(v)
				local n = tonumber(v)
				if not n then return end
				colaBoostValue = n
				pcall(function()
					for _, item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
						if item.Name == "ColaBoost" and item:IsA("NumberValue") then
							item.Value = colaBoostValue
						end
					end
				end)
				Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Cola Boost", Content = "Set to " .. tostring(n), Duration = 2 })
			end,
		})

		tab:AddInput("OldFolder_SpeedPad", {
			Title = "Speed Pad Boost Changer",
			Default = "1.3",
			Placeholder = "1.3",
			Callback = function(v)
				local n = tonumber(v)
				if not n then return end
				speedPadValue = n
				pcall(function()
					for _, item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
						if item.Name == "SpeedPad" and item:IsA("NumberValue") then
							item.Value = speedPadValue
						end
					end
				end)
				Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Speed Pad", Content = "Set to " .. tostring(n), Duration = 2 })
			end,
		})

		tab:AddSection("Player Speed")

		_G.__HXRealSpeedOverride = _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride or 1500

		local function applyRealSpeed(char)
			if not char then return end
			local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
			if not hum then return end
			local target = _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride
			pcall(function() hum:SetAttribute("RealSpeed", target) end)
			pcall(function()
				hum:GetAttributeChangedSignal("RealSpeed"):Connect(function()
					if hum:GetAttribute("RealSpeed") ~= _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride then
						hum:SetAttribute("RealSpeed", _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride)
					end
				end)
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local comm = char:FindFirstChild("Communicator")
				if comm and comm:IsA("RemoteEvent") and not comm:GetAttribute("HX_SpeedHooked") then
					comm:SetAttribute("HX_SpeedHooked", true)
					local oldFire = comm.FireServer
					comm.FireServer = function(self, ...)
						local args = {...}
						if args[1] == "update" then
							task.wait()
							if hum and hum.Parent then
								hum:SetAttribute("RealSpeed", _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride)
							end
							return _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride, hum and hum:GetAttribute("RealJumpHeight")
						end
						return oldFire(self, ...)
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		if LocalPlayer.Character then
			task.spawn(applyRealSpeed, LocalPlayer.Character)
		end
		LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
			task.spawn(applyRealSpeed, char)
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while true do
				task.wait(0.5)
				local char = LocalPlayer.Character
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum and hum:GetAttribute("RealSpeed") ~= _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride then
					pcall(function() hum:SetAttribute("RealSpeed", _G.__HXRealSpeedOverride) end)
				end
			end
		end)

		local speedSlider
		speedSlider = tab:AddSlider("OldFolder_PlayerSpeed", {
			Title = "Player Speed",
			Default = 1500,
			Min = 100,
			Max = 5000,
			Rounding = 0,
			Callback = function(v)
				_G.__HXRealSpeedOverride = v
				local char = LocalPlayer.Character
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum then pcall(function() hum:SetAttribute("RealSpeed", v) end) end
			end,
		})

		tab:AddInput("OldFolder_PlayerSpeedManual", {
			Title = "Player Speed (Manual)",
			Default = "1500",
			Placeholder = "1500",
			Callback = function(v)
				local n = tonumber(v)
				if not n then return end
				_G.__HXRealSpeedOverride = n
				local char = LocalPlayer.Character
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum then pcall(function() hum:SetAttribute("RealSpeed", n) end) end
				pcall(function()
					if speedSlider and speedSlider.SetValue then speedSlider:SetValue(n) end
				end)
				Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Player Speed", Content = "Set to " .. tostring(n), Duration = 2 })
			end,
		})

		-- GrappleHook <-> GrappleShot name swap
		tab:AddSection("Grapple Swap")
		local grappleSwapped = false

		local function setGrappleSwap(on)
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				local ws = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("WeaponStorage")
				if not ws then error("WeaponStorage not found") end

				local function get(name)
					return ws:FindFirstChild(name)
				end

				if on and not grappleSwapped then
					local h = get("GrappleHook")
					local s = get("GrappleShot")
					if not h or not s then error("GrappleHook/GrappleShot missing") end
					-- swap names via temp (same as provided snippet)
					h.Name, s.Name = "Temp", "GrappleHook"
					ws.Temp.Name = "GrappleShot"
					grappleSwapped = true
				elseif (not on) and grappleSwapped then
					-- reverse: current GrappleHook is original Shot, GrappleShot is original Hook
					local h = get("GrappleHook") -- was Shot
					local s = get("GrappleShot") -- was Hook
					if not h or not s then error("swapped weapons missing") end
					h.Name, s.Name = "Temp", "GrappleShot"
					ws.Temp.Name = "GrappleHook"
					grappleSwapped = false
				end
			end)
			return ok, err
		end

		tab:AddToggle("OldFolder_GrappleSwap", {
			Title = "GrappleHook → GrappleShot",
			Description = "Swaps GrappleHook and GrappleShot in WeaponStorage. Toggle off to restore.",
			Default = false,
			Callback = function(v)
				local ok, err = setGrappleSwap(v and true or false)
				if ok then
					Fluent:Notify({
						Title = "Grapple Swap",
						Content = v and "GrappleHook is now GrappleShot" or "Names restored",
						Duration = 3,
					})
				else
					Fluent:Notify({
						Title = "Grapple Swap",
						Content = "Failed: " .. tostring(err),
						Duration = 4,
					})
				end
			end,
		})
	end
end



;(function()
    local okChat, errChat = pcall(function()
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer

    local NTFY = "https://ntfy.sh/"
    local PREFIX = "hyperion-hx-v2-7f3k29q"
    local CHANNELS = {
        { id = "chat", label = "chat" },
    }
    local POLL_EVERY = 7
    local HEARTBEAT_EVERY = 30
    local ONLINE_WINDOW = 70
    local MAX_LINES = 60
    local MAX_TEXT = 200
    local CHAT_HEIGHT = math.clamp(((_G.__HXWin and _G.__HXWin.h) or 460) - 150, 200, 520)

    local requestFn = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request or (fluxus and fluxus.request)

    _G.__HXChatGen = (_G.__HXChatGen or 0) + 1
    local myGen = _G.__HXChatGen
    local alive = function() return _G.__HXChatGen == myGen end

    local myId = tostring(lp.UserId)
    local myName = lp.Name:gsub("[|\n\r]", ""):sub(1, 24)
    local OWNER_NAMES = { nymnmz = true, lucas_adm145t = true }
    local isOwner = OWNER_NAMES[myName:lower()] == true
    local myTier = (_G.__HXTier == "Premium") and "premium" or "free"
    local tiers = { [myId] = myTier }
    local mutedUntil  = {}
    local bannedUntil = {}
    local function isUserMuted(uid)  return mutedUntil[uid]  and os.time() < mutedUntil[uid] end
    local function isUserBanned(uid) return bannedUntil[uid] and (bannedUntil[uid] == -1 or os.time() < bannedUntil[uid]) end

    local presenceTopic = PREFIX .. "-presence"
    local sysTopic = PREFIX .. "-sys"
    local topicToChannel, topics = {}, { presenceTopic, sysTopic }
    local state = {
        ch = {},
        active = CHANNELS[1].id,
        sinceTs = nil,
        seenIds = {}, seenOrder = {},
        users = {},
        pending = {},
        failures = 0, backoff = 0, lastSent = 0,
        connected = false, loaded = false, online = 0,
    }
    for _, c in ipairs(CHANNELS) do
        c.topic = PREFIX .. "-" .. c.id
        topicToChannel[c.topic] = c.id
        topics[#topics + 1] = c.topic
        state.ch[c.id] = { log = {}, unread = 0, mention = false }
    end

    local function clean(s, max)
        s = tostring(s or ""):gsub("[%c<>]", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        return s:sub(1, max)
    end

    local function safeText(t)
        local l = t:lower()
        if l:find("http", 1, true) or l:find("www.", 1, true) or l:find("discord.gg", 1, true) or l:find(".com", 1, true) then
            return "[link removed]"
        end
        return t
    end

    local function http(method, url, body)
        if type(requestFn) ~= "function" then return nil, "no request function" end
        local headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "text/plain", ["Content-Length"] = tostring(#(body or "")) }
        local attempts = {
            { Url = url, Method = method, Body = body, Headers = headers },
            { url = url, method = method, body = body, headers = headers },
            { Url = url, Method = method, Body = body },
        }
        local lastErr = "request failed"
        for _, opts in ipairs(attempts) do
            local ok, res = pcall(requestFn, opts)
            if ok and type(res) == "table" then
                return res, nil
            end
            if not ok then lastErr = tostring(res) end
        end
        return nil, lastErr
    end
    local function publish(topic, payload)
        payload = tostring(payload or "")
        if payload == "" then return false, "empty payload" end
        local res, err = http("POST", NTFY .. topic, payload)
        if not res then return false, err or "network error" end
        local code = tonumber(res.StatusCode or res.status_code or res.Status or res.status) or 0
        if code >= 200 and code < 300 then return true, nil end
        local body = tostring(res.Body or res.body or "")
        return false, "HTTP " .. tostring(code) .. (body ~= "" and (": " .. body:sub(1, 80)) or "")
    end

    local function ownerNotify(title, content, duration)
        title = tostring(title or "Hyperion")
        content = tostring(content or "")
        duration = tonumber(duration) or 5
        local ok = pcall(function()
            Fluent:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = duration })
        end)
        if not ok then
            pcall(function()
                game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = title,
                    Text = content,
                    Duration = duration,
                })
            end)
        end
    end


    -- ── Owner Lockdown (broadcast via sys topic) ─────────────────
    local lockdownState = { active = false, reason = "Maintenance", by = "", since = 0 }
    _G.__HXIsOwner = isOwner
    _G.__HXGetLockdownState = function()
        return {
            active = lockdownState.active and true or false,
            reason = lockdownState.reason or "",
            by = lockdownState.by or "",
            since = lockdownState.since or 0,
        }
    end

    local function destroyLockdownGui()
        if _G.__HXLockdownGui then
            pcall(function() _G.__HXLockdownGui:Destroy() end)
            _G.__HXLockdownGui = nil
        end
    end

    local function unloadHyperionScript()
        pcall(function()
            local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
            if env.eu and env.eu.unload then env.eu:unload() end
        end)
        pcall(function() if _G.EvawareFarmCleanup then _G.EvawareFarmCleanup() end end)
        pcall(function() if Window and Window.Destroy then Window:Destroy() end end)
        pcall(function()
            local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            if not pg then return end
            for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
                if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                    local n = gui.Name
                    if n == "openshit" or n:find("HX_", 1, true) or n:find("Hyperion", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Fluent", 1, true) or n:find("Evaware", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local hui = type(gethui) == "function" and gethui()
            if not hui then return end
            for _, gui in ipairs(hui:GetChildren()) do
                if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                    local n = gui.Name
                    if n:find("HX_", 1, true) or n:find("Hyperion", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Fluent", 1, true) or n:find("Evaware", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        _G.__HXChatGen = (_G.__HXChatGen or 0) + 1
    end

    local function showLockdownScreen(reason, byName)
        if isOwner then return end
        if _G.__HXLockdownGui and _G.__HXLockdownGui.Parent then return end

        pcall(function()
            local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
            if not pg then return end
            destroyLockdownGui()

            local lockGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            lockGui.Name = "HX_LockdownScreen"
            lockGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            lockGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            lockGui.DisplayOrder = 200000
            lockGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            lockGui.Parent = pg
            _G.__HXLockdownGui = lockGui

            local accent = Color3.fromRGB(220, 35, 45)
            local accentSoft = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)

            local shade = Instance.new("Frame")
            shade.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            shade.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 2, 4)
            shade.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
            shade.BorderSizePixel = 0
            shade.Parent = lockGui

            local vignette = Instance.new("Frame")
            vignette.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            vignette.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 0, 10)
            vignette.BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
            vignette.BorderSizePixel = 0
            vignette.ZIndex = 1
            vignette.Parent = shade

            local card = Instance.new("Frame")
            card.Name = "Card"
            card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            card.Size = UDim2.new(0.88, 0, 0, 0)
            card.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 8, 10)
            card.BorderSizePixel = 0
            card.ZIndex = 3
            card.Parent = shade
            Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
            local cardStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            cardStroke.Color = accent
            cardStroke.Thickness = 2
            cardStroke.Transparency = 0.15
            cardStroke.Parent = card
            local cardGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            cardGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(180, 20, 30)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)),
            })
            cardGrad.Parent = cardStroke
            local cardPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            cardPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 22)
            cardPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 20)
            cardPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 22)
            cardPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 22)
            cardPad.Parent = card
            local cardLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            cardLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            cardLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 12)
            cardLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            cardLayout.Parent = card
            local cardSize = Instance.new("UISizeConstraint")
            cardSize.MaxSize = Vector2.new(420, 900)
            cardSize.Parent = card

            local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            icon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
            icon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            icon.TextSize = 28
            icon.Text = "⚠  LOCKDOWN"
            icon.TextColor3 = accentSoft
            icon.LayoutOrder = 1
            icon.Parent = card

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
            title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            title.TextSize = 20
            title.Text = "Hyperion Hub is locked"
            title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 230)
            title.LayoutOrder = 2
            title.Parent = card

            local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
            subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            subtitle.TextSize = 13
            subtitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 140, 145)
            subtitle.Text = "Script access suspended for all users"
            subtitle.LayoutOrder = 3
            subtitle.Parent = card

            local reasonBox = Instance.new("Frame")
            reasonBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            reasonBox.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            reasonBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 12, 16)
            reasonBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
            reasonBox.LayoutOrder = 4
            reasonBox.Parent = card
            Instance.new("UICorner", reasonBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
            local rbPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            rbPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12)
            rbPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12)
            rbPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14)
            rbPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14)
            rbPad.Parent = reasonBox

            local reasonTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            reasonTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            reasonTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
            reasonTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            reasonTitle.TextSize = 11
            reasonTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 120, 125)
            reasonTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            reasonTitle.Text = "REASON"
            reasonTitle.Parent = reasonBox

            local reasonLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            reasonLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            reasonLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18)
            reasonLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            reasonLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            reasonLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            reasonLabel.TextSize = 15
            reasonLabel.TextWrapped = true
            reasonLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            reasonLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            reasonLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 235)
            reasonLabel.Text = tostring(reason or "No reason provided")
            reasonLabel.Parent = reasonBox

            local byLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            byLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            byLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
            byLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            byLabel.TextSize = 12
            byLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 110, 115)
            byLabel.Text = "Issued by " .. tostring(byName or "Owner")
            byLabel.LayoutOrder = 5
            byLabel.Parent = card

            local logout = Instance.new("TextButton")
            logout.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
            logout.BackgroundColor3 = accent
            logout.BorderSizePixel = 0
            logout.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            logout.TextSize = 15
            logout.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            logout.Text = "Unload Hyperion"
            logout.AutoButtonColor = true
            logout.LayoutOrder = 6
            logout.Parent = card
            Instance.new("UICorner", logout).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

            pcall(function() if Window and Window.Root then Window.Root.Visible = false end end)
            for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
                if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui ~= lockGui then
                    local n = gui.Name
                    if n == "openshit" or n:find("HX_", 1, true) or n:find("Hyperion", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Fluent", 1, true) or n:find("Evaware", 1, true) then
                        gui.Enabled = false
                    end
                end
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = os.clock()
                while lockGui.Parent and _G.__HXLockdownGui == lockGui do
                    local t = os.clock() - t0
                    local pulse = (math.sin(t * 2.2) + 1) * 0.5
                    vignette.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 + pulse * 0.25
                    cardStroke.Transparency = 0.05 + pulse * 0.25
                    cardStroke.Thickness = 1.5 + pulse * 1.5
                    cardGrad.Rotation = (t * 70) % 360
                    icon.TextTransparency = pulse * 0.15
                    task.wait(0.04)
                end
            end)

            logout.Activated:Connect(function()
                pcall(function() lockGui:Destroy() end)
                _G.__HXLockdownGui = nil
                unloadHyperionScript()
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function applyLockdown(active, reason, byName)
        if isOwner then
            lockdownState.active = active and true or false
            lockdownState.reason = reason or lockdownState.reason or "Maintenance"
            lockdownState.by = byName or myName
            lockdownState.since = active and os.time() or 0
            return
        end
        if active then
            lockdownState.active = true
            lockdownState.reason = reason or "Maintenance"
            lockdownState.by = byName or "Owner"
            lockdownState.since = os.time()
            showLockdownScreen(lockdownState.reason, lockdownState.by)
            task.delay(12, function()
                if lockdownState.active and not isOwner then
                    if _G.__HXLockdownGui and _G.__HXLockdownGui.Parent then
                        pcall(function() _G.__HXLockdownGui:Destroy() end)
                        _G.__HXLockdownGui = nil
                    end
                    unloadHyperionScript()
                end
            end)
        else
            lockdownState.active = false
            destroyLockdownGui()
        end
    end

    local function publishLockdown(active, reason)
        if not isOwner then return false end
        reason = clean((reason or "Maintenance"):gsub("|", " "), 180)
        if reason == "" then reason = "Maintenance" end
        local flag = active and "1" or "0"
        local ok = publish(sysTopic, "L|" .. myId .. "|" .. myName .. "|" .. flag .. "|" .. reason)
        if ok then applyLockdown(active, reason, myName) end
        return ok
    end

    _G.__HXSetLockdown = function(active, reason)
        if not isOwner then return false, "Owners only" end
        return publishLockdown(active and true or false, reason)
    end

    local ChatTabObj = Tabs.Chat or Window:AddTab({ Title = "| Chat", Icon = "message-circle" })
    pcall(function() if _G.__HXRegisterTab then _G.__HXRegisterTab(ChatTabObj, "Chat", 13) end end)
    if not requestFn then
        ChatTabObj:AddParagraph({ Title = "Chat unavailable", Content = "Your executor has no HTTP request function." })
        return
    end

    local host
    do
        local ok, el = pcall(function() return ChatTabObj:AddParagraph({ Title = "", Content = "" }) end)
        local frame = ok and el and el.Frame
        if typeof(frame) == "Instance" then host = frame end
    end

    local S = {
        surface = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), surfaceT = 0.92,
        stroke = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), strokeT = 0.88,
        text = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240), sub = Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 170),
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(96, 205, 255),
        green = Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 140), red = Color3.fromRGB(235, 70, 80), gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 90),
    }
    do
        local accents = {
            Dark = Color3.fromRGB(96, 205, 255), Darker = Color3.fromRGB(72, 138, 182), Light = Color3.fromRGB(0, 103, 192),
            Aqua = Color3.fromRGB(60, 165, 165), Amethyst = Color3.fromRGB(85, 57, 139), Rose = Color3.fromRGB(180, 55, 90),
        }
        pcall(function()
            local name = Fluent.Theme
            if type(name) == "string" and accents[name] then S.accent = accents[name] end
        end)
        if host then
            pcall(function()
                if host.BackgroundTransparency < 0.99 then
                    S.surface, S.surfaceT = host.BackgroundColor3, host.BackgroundTransparency
                end
                local st = host:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if st then S.stroke, S.strokeT = st.Color, st.Transparency; st.Enabled = false end
                host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end)
        end
    end

    local NAME_COLORS = {
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 105), Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 90), Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 150),
        Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255), Color3.fromRGB(170, 150, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 190),
    }
    local function colorFor(uid)
        local n = 0
        for i = 1, #uid do n = (n * 31 + uid:byte(i)) % 997 end
        return NAME_COLORS[n % #NAME_COLORS + 1]
    end
    local function new(class, props, parent)
        local o = __Inew(class)
        for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
        if parent then o.Parent = parent end
        return o
    end
    local function round(inst, r) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, inst) end
    local function pad(inst, l, t, r, b)
        return new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b) }, inst)
    end
    local function outline(inst, transparency)
        return new("UIStroke", { Color = S.stroke, Transparency = transparency or S.strokeT, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, inst)
    end
    local function setFont(o, weight)
        local ok = pcall(function()
            o.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", weight or Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
        end)
        if not ok then o.Font = (weight == Enum.FontWeight.Bold or weight == Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold) and Enum.Font.GothamBold or Enum.Font.Gotham end
    end
    local function label(props, parent)
        local weight = props.Weight
        props.Weight = nil
        if props.BackgroundTransparency == nil then props.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
        props.TextColor3 = props.TextColor3 or S.text
        props.TextSize = props.TextSize or 13
        local o = new("TextLabel", props, parent)
        setFont(o, weight)
        return o
    end
    local function scroller(props, parent)
        props.Name = "HXScroll"
        props.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        props.BorderSizePixel = 0
        props.Active = true
        props.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
        props.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        props.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        props.ScrollingEnabled = true
        props.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never
        props.ScrollBarThickness = props.ScrollBarThickness or 3
        props.ScrollBarImageColor3 = S.sub
        props.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4
        props.VerticalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.ScrollBar
        return new("ScrollingFrame", props, parent)
    end

        TextColor3 = S.text, PlaceholderText = "Say something...", PlaceholderColor3 = S.sub,

    setFont(sendBtn, Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)
    round(sendBtn, 5)
    local clearRows

    if isOwner then
        local clearBtn = new("TextButton", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 28), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(65, 65, 82),
            BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = true, TextSize = 11,

        }, inputRow)

        round(clearBtn, 5)
        clearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            for _, c in ipairs(CHANNELS) do
                publish(PREFIX .. "-" .. c.id, "X|" .. myId .. "|" .. myName)
            end

            end
            clearRows()
