        end)
    end

    local rows, rowCounter = {}, 0
    local function atBottom()
        return list.CanvasPosition.Y >= list.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - list.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y - 30
    end
    local function scrollBottom()
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait()
            list.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, list.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - list.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y))
            newBtn.Visible = false
        end)
    end
    list:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
        if newBtn.Visible and atBottom() then newBtn.Visible = false end
    end)
    newBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(scrollBottom)

    local function addRow(m)
        emptyLabel.Visible = false
        local mine = m.uid == myId
        rowCounter += 1
        local row = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = rowCounter }, list)
        local nameColor = mine and S.accent or colorFor(m.uid)

        local avatar = new("ImageLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(mine and 1 or 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(mine and 1 or 0, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0,
            BackgroundColor3 = nameColor, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = "",
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop,
        }, row)
        round(avatar, 13)
        if isOwner and not mine then
            local ctxHit = new("TextButton", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                Text = "", ZIndex = avatar.ZIndex + 2, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, avatar)
            ctxHit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if showCtxMenu then showCtxMenu(m.uid, m.name, avatar.AbsolutePosition) end
            end)
        end
        local avatarFallback = label({ Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Bold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20),
            Text = (m.name:sub(1, 1)):upper(), ZIndex = 2 }, avatar)
        task.spawn(function()
            local uid = tonumber(m.uid)
            if not uid or not avatar.Parent then return end
            local ok, imageUrl, ready = pcall(function()
                return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(uid, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48)
            end)
            if ok and avatar.Parent and type(imageUrl) == "string" and imageUrl ~= "" then
                avatar.Image = imageUrl
                avatarFallback.Visible = false
            end
        end)

        local body = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(mine and 1 or 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(mine and 1 or 0, mine and -34 or 34, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0, 0),
        }, row)
        new("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), HorizontalAlignment = mine and Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left }, body)

        local header = new("Frame", { LayoutOrder = 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16) }, body)
        new("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6) }, header)
        label({ LayoutOrder = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY, Weight = Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold,
            TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = nameColor, Text = m.name }, header)
        local ownerNames = { nymnmz = true, lucas_adm145t = true }
        if ownerNames[tostring(m.name):lower()] then
            local ownerTag = label({ LayoutOrder = 2, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 105, 35), TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Bold, TextSize = 9, Text = "OWNER" }, header)
            round(ownerTag, 4)
            pad(ownerTag, 5, 1, 5, 1)
        end
        if tiers[tostring(m.uid)] == "premium" then
            local premTag = label({ LayoutOrder = 2, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50), TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 42, 0),
                Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Bold, TextSize = 9, Text = "PREMIUM" }, header)
            round(premTag, 4)
            pad(premTag, 5, 1, 5, 1)
        end
        label({ LayoutOrder = 3, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY, TextSize = 10,
            TextColor3 = S.sub, Text = os.date("%H:%M", m.t) }, header)

        local mentioned = (not mine) and m.text:lower():find("@" .. myName:lower(), 1, true) ~= nil
        local bubble = label({
            LayoutOrder = 2, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
            BackgroundTransparency = mine and 0.15 or S.surfaceT, BackgroundColor3 = mine and S.accent or S.surface,
            TextColor3 = mine and Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15) or S.text,
            TextWrapped = true, RichText = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = m.text,
        }, body)
        round(bubble, 6)
        pad(bubble, 10, 6, 10, 6)
        new("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(250, math.huge) }, bubble)
        if mentioned then new("UIStroke", { Color = S.gold, Thickness = 1.5 }, bubble)
        elseif not mine then outline(bubble) end
        return row
    end

    clearRows = function()
        for _, r in ipairs(rows) do r:Destroy() end
        table.clear(rows)
        emptyLabel.Visible = true
    end

    local function renderActive()
        clearRows()
        for _, m in ipairs(state.ch[state.active].log) do rows[#rows + 1] = addRow(m) end
        scrollBottom()
    end

    local function pushMessage(chId, m)
        if m.uid ~= myId and (isUserMuted(m.uid) or isUserBanned(m.uid)) then return end
        local ch = state.ch[chId]
        ch.log[#ch.log + 1] = m
        while #ch.log > MAX_LINES do table.remove(ch.log, 1) end
        if chId == state.active then
            local wasBottom = atBottom()
            rows[#rows + 1] = addRow(m)
            while #rows > MAX_LINES do
                local r = table.remove(rows, 1)
                if r then r:Destroy() end
            end
            if wasBottom or m.uid == myId then scrollBottom() else newBtn.Visible = true end
        end
    end

    if isOwner then
        local ctxTargetUid, ctxTargetName = nil, nil

        local ctxMenu = new("Frame", {
            Name = "HXCtxMenu",
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 26),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.04,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(154, 138),
            Visible = false,
            ZIndex = 30,
        }, main)
        round(ctxMenu, 7)
        outline(ctxMenu, 0.45)
        pad(ctxMenu, 7, 7, 7, 7)
        new("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 5) }, ctxMenu)

        local ctxNameLabel = label({
            LayoutOrder = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14),
            Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Bold,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextColor3 = S.accent,
            Text = "",
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ZIndex = 31,
        }, ctxMenu)

        local function makeActionRow(order, prefix, btnDefs, btnBg, btnTc, callback)
            local row = new("Frame", { LayoutOrder = order, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), ZIndex = 31 }, ctxMenu)
            label({ Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 14), Text = prefix, TextSize = 10,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextColor3 = S.sub, ZIndex = 31 }, row)
            local bf = new("Frame", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 31 }, row)
            new("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center }, bf)
            for i, def in ipairs(btnDefs) do
                local b = new("TextButton", {
                    LayoutOrder = i, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.88, 0), BackgroundColor3 = btnBg,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = true, TextSize = 10,
                    TextColor3 = btnTc, Text = def[1], ZIndex = 31,
                }, bf)
                round(b, 4); pad(b, 5, 0, 5, 0)
                local secs = def[2]
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    callback(secs, def[1])
                    ctxMenu.Visible = false
                end)
            end
        end

        makeActionRow(2, "Mute:", {
            { "5m",  300 }, { "30m", 1800 }, { "1h", 3600 },
        }, Color3.fromRGB(42, 76, 128), Color3.fromRGB(175, 210, 255),
        function(secs, label_)
            if ctxTargetUid then
                mutedUntil[ctxTargetUid] = os.time() + secs
                Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Chat", Content = (ctxTargetName or "User") .. " muted for " .. label_, Duration = 3 })
            end
        end)

        makeActionRow(3, "Ban:", {
            { "1h", 3600 }, { "24h", 86400 }, { "Perm", -1 },
        }, Color3.fromRGB(100, 28, 38), Color3.fromRGB(255, 175, 175),
        function(secs, label_)
            if ctxTargetUid then
                bannedUntil[ctxTargetUid] = (secs == -1) and -1 or (os.time() + secs)
                Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Chat", Content = (ctxTargetName or "User") .. " banned for " .. label_, Duration = 3 })
            end
        end)

        local closeFrame = new("Frame", { LayoutOrder = 4, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), ZIndex = 31 }, ctxMenu)
        local closeBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 31,
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 50), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AutoButtonColor = true, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = S.sub, Text = "✕  Close" }, closeFrame)
        round(closeBtn, 4)
        closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() ctxMenu.Visible = false end)

        main.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
            if ctxMenu.Visible and inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                local mp = inp.Position
                local cp = ctxMenu.AbsolutePosition
                local cs = ctxMenu.AbsoluteSize
                if mp.X < cp.X or mp.X > cp.X + cs.X or mp.Y < cp.Y or mp.Y > cp.Y + cs.Y then
                    ctxMenu.Visible = false
                end
            end
        end)

        showCtxMenu = function(uid, name, absPos)
            ctxTargetUid  = uid
            ctxTargetName = name
            ctxNameLabel.Text = "@" .. name
            local relX = absPos.X - main.AbsolutePosition.X
            local relY = absPos.Y - main.AbsolutePosition.Y + 30
            local mw, mh = 154, 138
            ctxMenu.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
                math.clamp(relX, 0, math.max(0, main.AbsoluteSize.X - mw)),
                math.clamp(relY, 0, math.max(0, main.AbsoluteSize.Y - mh))
            )
            ctxMenu.Visible = true
        end
    end

    local function refreshChannels() end

    local function setActive(id)
        state.active = id
        state.ch[id].unread, state.ch[id].mention = 0, false
        for _, c in ipairs(CHANNELS) do
            if c.id == id then
            end
        end
        refreshChannels()
        renderActive()
    end

    local floatGui
    if host then
        for _, ch in ipairs(host:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextLabel") or ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch.Visible = false end
        end
        pcall(function()
            host.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
            host.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, CHAT_HEIGHT)
        end)
        root.Parent = host
    else
        floatGui = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "HX_ChatWindow", ResetOnSpawn = false, DisplayOrder = 40 })
        local ok = pcall(function() floatGui.Parent = (type(gethui) == "function" and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        if not ok then floatGui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
        root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        root.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(500, 360)
        root.Visible = false
        root.Parent = floatGui
        ChatTabObj:AddToggle("HXChatWindowToggle", {
            Title = "Show chat",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(v) root.Visible = v and true or false end,
        })
    end

    local function refreshUsers()
        local now, n = os.time(), 0
        state.users[myId] = { name = myName, t = now }
        for uid, u in pairs(state.users) do
            if now - u.t <= ONLINE_WINDOW then n += 1 else state.users[uid] = nil end
        end
        state.online = n
    end

    local function setOnlineUI()
        local n = state.online
        _G.__HXOnlineCount = state.connected and n or nil
        if type(_G.__HXSetOnline) == "function" then pcall(_G.__HXSetOnline, state.connected and n or nil) end
        statusDot.BackgroundColor3 = state.connected and S.green or S.sub
        statusText.Text = state.connected and (n .. " online") or "Offline"
    end

    local function handleEvent(ev)
        if type(ev) ~= "table" or ev.event ~= "message" or type(ev.message) ~= "string" or type(ev.id) ~= "string" then return end
        if state.seenIds[ev.id] then return end
        state.seenIds[ev.id] = true
        state.seenOrder[#state.seenOrder + 1] = ev.id
        if #state.seenOrder > 800 then state.seenIds[table.remove(state.seenOrder, 1)] = nil end

        local t = tonumber(ev.time) or os.time()
        if not state.maxTs or t > state.maxTs then state.maxTs = t end

        local kind, uid, rest = ev.message:match("^(%u)|(%d+)|(.*)$")
        if not kind or #uid > 20 then return end

        if ev.topic == presenceTopic and kind == "P" then
            local pname, ptier = rest:match("^([^|]*)|?(.*)$")
            local name = clean(pname or "", 24)
            ptier = (ptier == "premium") and "premium" or "free"
            if name ~= "" then
                local u = state.users[uid]
                if not u or t >= u.t then state.users[uid] = { name = name, t = t, tier = ptier } end
                if tiers[uid] ~= ptier and uid ~= myId then
                    tiers[uid] = ptier
                    state.tierDirty = true
                end
            end
            return
        end

        if ev.topic == sysTopic then
            if kind == "L" then
                if t < os.time() - 86400 then return end
                -- payload: L|senderId|senderName|0or1|reason  -> rest = senderName|0or1|reason
                local name, flag, reason = rest:match("^([^|]*)|([01])|(.*)$")
                name = clean(name or "", 24)
                if name == "" or not OWNER_NAMES[name:lower()] then return end
                local active = (flag == "1")
                reason = safeText(clean(reason or "Maintenance", 180))
                if reason == "" then reason = "Maintenance" end
                applyLockdown(active, reason, name)
                return
            end
            if t < os.time() - 120 then return end
            if kind == "D" then
                -- Targeted moderation notice: D|senderName|targetUserId|action|durationSeconds|reason
                local name, targetUid, action, duration, reason = rest:match("^([^|]*)|(%d+)|([^|]*)|([^|]*)|(.*)$")
                name = clean(name or "", 24)
                if not OWNER_NAMES[name:lower()] or targetUid ~= myId then return end
                action = clean(action or "Moderation", 24)
                reason = safeText(clean(reason or "No reason provided", 180))
                duration = tonumber(duration) or 0
                local sub = (action == "Timeout") and ("Timeout · " .. tostring(math.max(1, math.floor(duration / 60))) .. " min") or action
                pcall(function()
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Hyperion · " .. sub,
                        Content = "Reason: " .. reason,
                        SubContent = "Moderation notice from " .. name,
                        Duration = 14,
                    })
                end)
                state.moderationNotice = { action = action, reason = reason, untilTime = (action == "Timeout" and duration > 0) and (os.time() + duration) or nil }


                -- Client lockout for Timeout/Ban
                if action == "Timeout" or action == "Ban" then
                    pcall(function()
                        local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
                        if not pg then return end
                        if _G.__HXModerationLockGui then pcall(function() _G.__HXModerationLockGui:Destroy() end) end

                        local lockGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                        lockGui.Name = "HX_ModerationLockout"
                        lockGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
                        lockGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
                        lockGui.DisplayOrder = 100000
                        lockGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
                        lockGui.Parent = pg
                        _G.__HXModerationLockGui = lockGui

                        local isBan = (action == "Ban")
                        local accent = isBan and Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 40)

                        local shade = Instance.new("Frame")
                        shade.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                        shade.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(6, 4, 8)
                        shade.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                        shade.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        shade.Parent = lockGui

                        local vignette = Instance.new("Frame")
                        vignette.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                        vignette.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                        vignette.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
                        vignette.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        vignette.ZIndex = 1
                        vignette.Parent = shade

                        -- Responsive card (scales with screen)
                        local card = Instance.new("Frame")
                        card.Name = "Card"
                        card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                        card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                        card.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 0)
                        card.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 14, 20)
                        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        card.ZIndex = 2
                        card.Parent = shade
                        local cardCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                        cardCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
                        cardCorner.Parent = card
                        local cardStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                        cardStroke.Color = accent
                        cardStroke.Thickness = 1.5
                        cardStroke.Transparency = 0.25
                        cardStroke.Parent = card
                        local cardPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                        cardPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 22)
                        cardPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 20)
                        cardPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 22)
                        cardPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 22)
                        cardPad.Parent = card
                        local cardSize = Instance.new("UISizeConstraint")
                        cardSize.MinSize = Vector2.new(280, 0)
                        cardSize.MaxSize = Vector2.new(420, 900)
                        cardSize.Parent = card
                        local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                        list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                        list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 12)
                        list.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
                        list.Parent = card

                        local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        icon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
                        icon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        icon.TextSize = 28
                        icon.Text = isBan and "⛔" or "⏱"
                        icon.LayoutOrder = 1
                        icon.Parent = card

                        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
                        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        title.TextSize = 20
                        title.TextColor3 = accent
                        title.Text = isBan and "Access disabled" or "Temporarily locked"
                        title.LayoutOrder = 2
                        title.Parent = card

                        local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
                        subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                        subtitle.TextSize = 13
                        subtitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 170, 185)
                        subtitle.Text = isBan and "Hyperion Hub has been banned on this client" or "Hyperion Hub is on timeout"
                        subtitle.LayoutOrder = 3
                        subtitle.Parent = card

                        local reasonBox = Instance.new("Frame")
                        reasonBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                        reasonBox.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                        reasonBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 22, 30)
                        reasonBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        reasonBox.LayoutOrder = 4
                        reasonBox.Parent = card
                        local rbCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                        rbCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
                        rbCorner.Parent = reasonBox
                        local rbPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                        rbPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12)
                        rbPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12)
                        rbPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14)
                        rbPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14)
                        rbPad.Parent = reasonBox

                        local reasonTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        reasonTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        reasonTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
                        reasonTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                        reasonTitle.TextSize = 11
                        reasonTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 150, 165)
                        reasonTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        reasonTitle.Text = "REASON"
                        reasonTitle.Parent = reasonBox

                        local reasonLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        reasonLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        reasonLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18)
                        reasonLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                        reasonLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                        reasonLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                        reasonLabel.TextSize = 15
                        reasonLabel.TextWrapped = true
                        reasonLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        reasonLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
                        reasonLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 235, 245)
                        reasonLabel.Text = reason
                        reasonLabel.Parent = reasonBox

                        local timerLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        timerLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        timerLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
                        timerLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        timerLabel.TextSize = 16
                        timerLabel.TextColor3 = accent
                        timerLabel.LayoutOrder = 5
                        timerLabel.Visible = (not isBan) and duration > 0
                        timerLabel.Text = ""
                        timerLabel.Parent = card

                        local logout = Instance.new("TextButton")
                        logout.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
                        logout.BackgroundColor3 = accent
                        logout.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        logout.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        logout.TextSize = 15
                        logout.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                        logout.Text = "Unload Hyperion"
                        logout.AutoButtonColor = true
                        logout.LayoutOrder = 6
                        logout.Parent = card
                        local bc = Instance.new("UICorner")
                        bc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
                        bc.Parent = logout

                        logout.Activated:Connect(function()
                            _G.__HXChatGen = (_G.__HXChatGen or 0) + 1
                            pcall(function() if Window and Window.Root then Window.Root:Destroy() end end)
                            pcall(function() if _G.__HXModerationLockGui then _G.__HXModerationLockGui:Destroy() end end)
                            pcall(function() local env = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G; if env.eu and env.eu.unload then env.eu:unload() end end)
                            pcall(function() if _G.EvawareFarmCleanup then _G.EvawareFarmCleanup() end end)
                            pcall(function() if Window and Window.Destroy then Window:Destroy() end end)
                            _G.__HXModerationLockGui = nil
                        end)

                        pcall(function() if Window and Window.Root then Window.Root.Visible = false end end)
                        for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
                            if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui ~= lockGui and gui.Name:find("HX_", 1, true) then
                                gui.Enabled = false
                            end
                        end

                        if action == "Timeout" and duration > 0 then
                            local endsAt = os.clock() + duration
                            task.spawn(function()
                                while lockGui.Parent and _G.__HXModerationLockGui == lockGui do
                                    local left = math.max(0, math.ceil(endsAt - os.clock()))
                                    local m = math.floor(left / 60)
                                    local s = left % 60
                                    timerLabel.Text = string.format("Unlocks in %02d:%02d", m, s)
                                    if left <= 0 then break end
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                end
                            end)
                            task.delay(duration, function()
                                if _G.__HXModerationLockGui == lockGui and lockGui.Parent then
                                    pcall(function() lockGui:Destroy() end)
                                    _G.__HXModerationLockGui = nil
                                    pcall(function() if Window and Window.Root then Window.Root.Visible = true end end)
                                    local pg2 = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                                    if pg2 then
                                        for _, gui in ipairs(pg2:GetChildren()) do
                                            if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Name:find("HX_", 1, true) then
                                                gui.Enabled = true
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                end

                return
            end
            if kind ~= "N" then return end
            -- payload: N|senderId|senderName|level|title|text  -> rest = senderName|level|title|text
            local name, level, title, text = rest:match("^([^|]*)|([^|]*)|([^|]*)|(.*)$")
            name = clean(name or "", 24)
            level = tostring(level or "info"):lower()
            if name == "" or not OWNER_NAMES[name:lower()] then return end
            title = clean(title or "", 40)
            text = safeText(clean(text or "", 220))
            if text == "" then return end
            if title == "" then title = "Announcement" end
            local icons = { info = "[i] ", warn = "[!] ", ok = "[OK] ", success = "[OK] ", warning = "[!] " }
            local prefix = icons[level] or "[i] "
            ownerNotify(prefix .. title, text .. "\n— " .. name, 10)
            return
        end

        local chId = topicToChannel[ev.topic]

        if chId and kind == "X" then
            local name = clean(rest, 24)
            if OWNER_NAMES[name:lower()] then
                if state.ch[chId] then table.clear(state.ch[chId].log) end
                if chId == state.active then clearRows() end
            end
            return
        end

        if chId and kind == "M" then
            local name, text = rest:match("^([^|]*)|(.*)$")
            name, text = clean(name, 24), clean(text, MAX_TEXT)
            if name == "" or text == "" then return end
            if uid == myId then
                for i, p in ipairs(state.pending) do
                    if p.ch == chId and p.text == text then
                        table.remove(state.pending, i)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
            text = safeText(text)
            pushMessage(chId, { uid = uid, name = name, text = text, t = t })
            if state.loaded and chId ~= state.active and uid ~= myId then
                local ch = state.ch[chId]
                ch.unread += 1
                if text:lower():find("@" .. myName:lower(), 1, true) then ch.mention = true end
                refreshChannels()
            end
        end
    end

    local function poll()
        local since = state.maxTs and tostring(state.maxTs - 1) or "30m"
        local res = http("GET", NTFY .. table.concat(topics, ",") .. "/json?poll=1&since=" .. since)
        if res and res.StatusCode == 200 then
            state.failures = 0
            state.connected = true
            for line in tostring(res.Body or ""):gmatch("[^\n]+") do
                local ok, ev = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, line)
                if ok then handleEvent(ev) end
            end
            state.loaded = true
            refreshUsers()
            setOnlineUI()
            if state.tierDirty then state.tierDirty = false; pcall(renderActive) end
            return
        end
        state.failures += 1
        if res and res.StatusCode == 429 then state.backoff = 20 end
        if state.failures >= 3 then
            state.connected = false
            setOnlineUI()
        end
    end

    local function sendMessage()
        local text = clean(box.Text, MAX_TEXT)
        if text == "" then return end
        if os.clock() - state.lastSent < 2 then
            Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Chat", Content = "Slow down a little", Duration = 2 })
            return
        end
        state.lastSent = os.clock()
        box.Text = ""
        local chId = state.active
        state.pending[#state.pending + 1] = { ch = chId, text = text }
        pushMessage(chId, { uid = myId, name = myName, text = text, t = os.time() })
        task.spawn(function()
            if not publish(PREFIX .. "-" .. chId, "M|" .. myId .. "|" .. myName .. "|" .. text) then
                Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Chat", Content = "Could not send your message", Duration = 3 })
            end
        end)
    end

    sendBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(sendMessage)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then sendMessage() end end)

    if isOwner then
        pcall(function()
            local panelGui, panelFrame, panelList, panelTitle
            local panelOpen = false
            local selectedUid, selectedName = nil, nil
            local mod = { action = "Ban", reason = "", last = 0 }

            local function ago(s)
                s = math.max(0, math.floor(s))
                if s < 60 then return s .. "s ago" end
                return math.floor(s / 60) .. "m ago"
            end

            local function fillPanel()
                if not (panelList and panelList.Parent) then return end
                refreshUsers()
                for _, c in ipairs(panelList:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end
                end
                local arr, now = {}, os.time()
                for uid, u in pairs(state.users) do
                    if now - u.t <= ONLINE_WINDOW then arr[#arr + 1] = { uid = uid, name = u.name, t = u.t } end
                end
                table.sort(arr, function(a, b)
                    local ao, bo = OWNER_NAMES[a.name:lower()] and 0 or 1, OWNER_NAMES[b.name:lower()] and 0 or 1
                    if ao ~= bo then return ao < bo end
                    return a.name:lower() < b.name:lower()
                end)
                panelTitle.Text = "Online users  (" .. #arr .. ")"
                for i, u in ipairs(arr) do
                    local row = new("Frame", { LayoutOrder = i, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundColor3 = S.surface, BackgroundTransparency = S.surfaceT, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, panelList)
                    round(row, 6)
                    local dot = new("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 12), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), BackgroundColor3 = S.green, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, row)
                    round(dot, 3)
                    local isMe = (u.uid == myId)
                    local isOwn = OWNER_NAMES[u.name:lower()]
                    label({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0), Text = u.name .. (isMe and "  (you)" or ""),
                        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                        TextColor3 = isOwn and S.gold or colorFor(u.uid), Weight = Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold }, row)
                    label({ AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 30),
                        Text = (isOwn and "OWNER · " or "") .. ((tiers[u.uid] == "premium") and "PREMIUM · " or "") .. ago(now - u.t), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = S.sub,
                        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right }, row)
                    local choose = new("TextButton", { Name = "SelectUser", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 5 }, row)
                    choose.Activated:Connect(function()
                        selectedUid, selectedName = u.uid, u.name
                        panelTitle.Text = "Selected: " .. u.name .. " (" .. u.uid .. ")"
                        Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Owner moderation", Content = "Selected " .. u.name .. " · UserId " .. u.uid, Duration = 3 })
                    end)
                end
                if #arr == 0 then
                    local row = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, panelList)
                    label({ Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Text = "Nobody online yet", TextColor3 = S.sub }, row)
                end
            end

            local function buildPanel()
                panelGui = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "HX_OnlineUsers", ResetOnSpawn = false, DisplayOrder = 45, IgnoreGuiInset = true })
                local ok = pcall(function() panelGui.Parent = (type(gethui) == "function" and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
                if not ok or not panelGui.Parent then panelGui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

                panelFrame = new("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 340),
                    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 26), BackgroundTransparency = 0.04, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = true,
                }, panelGui)
                round(panelFrame, 10)
                outline(panelFrame, 0.7)

                panelTitle = label({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 38), Text = "Online users",
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextSize = 14, Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Bold }, panelFrame)
                local close = new("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26),
                    BackgroundColor3 = S.surface, BackgroundTransparency = S.surfaceT, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "X", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = S.text, AutoButtonColor = true }, panelFrame)
                setFont(close, Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
                round(close, 6)
                close.Activated:Connect(function() panelOpen = false; panelFrame.Visible = false end)
                new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = S.stroke, BackgroundTransparency = S.strokeT, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 38), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1) }, panelFrame)

                panelList = scroller({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 42), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -48), ScrollBarThickness = 4 }, panelFrame)
                pad(panelList, 10, 4, 10, 4)
                new("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 5) }, panelList)

                local dragging, dragStart, startPos
                panelFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        local rel = input.Position.Y - panelFrame.AbsolutePosition.Y
                        if rel <= 38 then
                            dragging, dragStart, startPos = true, input.Position, panelFrame.Position
                            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
                UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                        local d = input.Position - dragStart
                        panelFrame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                    end
                end)

                task.spawn(function()
                    while alive() and panelGui and panelGui.Parent do
                        if panelOpen then pcall(fillPanel) end
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                    if panelGui then panelGui:Destroy() end
                end)
            end

            local function showPanel()
                if not panelGui or not panelGui.Parent then buildPanel() end
                panelOpen = true
                panelFrame.Visible = true
                fillPanel()
            end

            local bc = { title = "", text = "", level = "info", last = 0 }

            ChatTabObj:AddParagraph({
                Title = "Owner tools",
                Content = "Only owners see this. Check who is online and send a notification to every user running the script.",
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddButton({
                Title = "Show online users",
                Description = "Live list of everyone using Hyperion right now; tap a row to select a user",
                Callback = showPanel,
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddDropdown("HXModerationAction", {
                Title = "Moderation action",
                Values = { "Warn", "Timeout 10 min", "Timeout 1 hour", "Ban" },
                Multi = false,
                Default = "Ban",
                Callback = function(v)
                    if type(v) == "table" then v = v[1] end
                    mod.action = tostring(v or "Ban")
                end,
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddInput("HXModerationReason", {
                Title = "Moderation reason",
                Default = "",
                Placeholder = "Reason shown to the selected user",
                Numeric = false, Finished = false,
                Callback = function(v) mod.reason = tostring(v or "") end,
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddButton({
                Title = "Send moderation notice",
                Description = "Sends Warn / Timeout / Ban and the reason to the selected online Hyperion user",
                Callback = function()
                    if not selectedUid or selectedUid == myId then
                        Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Moderation", Content = "Select another user from the online list first", Duration = 4 })
                        return
                    end
                    local reason = clean((mod.reason or ""):gsub("|", " "), 180)
                    if reason == "" then
                        Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Moderation", Content = "Enter a reason first", Duration = 4 })
                        return
                    end
                    if os.clock() - mod.last < 5 then
                        Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Moderation", Content = "Wait a few seconds before sending another action", Duration = 3 })
                        return
                    end
                    local action, duration = mod.action or "Ban", 0
                    if action == "Timeout 10 min" then action, duration = "Timeout", 600
                    elseif action == "Timeout 1 hour" then action, duration = "Timeout", 3600
                    elseif action == "Warn" then action, duration = "Warn", 0
                    else action, duration = "Ban", -1 end
                    mod.last = os.clock()
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local payload = "D|" .. myId .. "|" .. myName .. "|" .. selectedUid .. "|" .. action .. "|" .. tostring(duration) .. "|" .. reason
                        local ok = publish(sysTopic, payload)
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Moderation",
                            Content = ok and (action .. " notice sent to " .. (selectedName or selectedUid)) or "Could not send (network)",
                            Duration = 4,
                        })
                    end)
                end,
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddInput("HXBroadcastTitle", {
                Title = "Notification title",
                Default = "",
                Placeholder = "Announcement",
                Numeric = false, Finished = false,
                Callback = function(v) bc.title = tostring(v or "") end,
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddInput("HXBroadcastText", {
                Title = "Notification message",
                Default = "",
                Placeholder = "Type the message everyone will see",
                Numeric = false, Finished = false,
                Callback = function(v) bc.text = tostring(v or "") end,
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddDropdown("HXBroadcastLevel", {
                Title = "Notification style",
                Values = { "Info", "Warning", "Success" },
                Multi = false,
                Default = "Info",
                Callback = function(v)
                    if type(v) == "table" then v = v[1] end
                    bc.level = (v == "Warning" and "warn") or (v == "Success" and "ok") or "info"
                end,
            })
            ChatTabObj:AddButton({
                Title = "Send notification to all users",
                Description = "Every online user of the script gets it within a few seconds",
                Callback = function()
                    local function readOpt(flag, fallback)
                        local ok, val = pcall(function()
                            if Options and Options[flag] and Options[flag].Value ~= nil then
                                return tostring(Options[flag].Value)
                            end
                            return nil
                        end)
                        if ok and type(val) == "string" and val ~= "" then return val end
                        return fallback
                    end

                    local title = clean((readOpt("HXBroadcastTitle", bc.title or "") or ""):gsub("|", " "), 40)
                    local text = clean((readOpt("HXBroadcastText", bc.text or "") or ""):gsub("|", " "), 220)
                    local level = tostring(bc.level or "info")
                    pcall(function()
                        local v = Options and Options["HXBroadcastLevel"] and Options["HXBroadcastLevel"].Value
                        if type(v) == "table" then v = v[1] or v[next(v)] end
                        if type(v) == "string" then
                            level = (v == "Warning" and "warn") or (v == "Success" and "ok") or "info"
                            bc.level = level
                        end
                    end)

                    if text == "" then
                        ownerNotify("Broadcast", "Write a message first", 3)
                        return
                    end
                    if title == "" then title = "Announcement" end
                    if os.clock() - (bc.last or 0) < 5 then
                        ownerNotify("Broadcast", "Wait a few seconds between notifications", 3)
                        return
                    end
                    bc.last = os.clock()
                    bc.title, bc.text = title, text

                    task.spawn(function()
                        local payload = "N|" .. tostring(myId) .. "|" .. tostring(myName) .. "|" .. level .. "|" .. title .. "|" .. text
                        local ok, err = false, nil
                        local okCall, a, b = pcall(function()
                            return publish(sysTopic, payload)
                        end)
                        if okCall then
                            ok, err = a, b
                        else
                            ok, err = false, tostring(a)
                        end
                        pcall(refreshUsers)
                        if ok then
                            ownerNotify("Broadcast", "Sent to " .. math.max(0, (state.online or 1) - 1) .. " online user(s)", 4)
                            -- Show a local preview so owner also sees it immediately
                            ownerNotify("[i] " .. title, text, 8)
                        else
                            ownerNotify("Broadcast failed", tostring(err or "network error"), 6)
                        end
                    end)
                end,
            })
        end)
    end

    setActive(state.active)
    refreshUsers()

    task.spawn(function()
        local lastBeat = 0
        while alive() do
            if os.clock() - lastBeat >= HEARTBEAT_EVERY then
                lastBeat = os.clock()
                publish(presenceTopic, "P|" .. myId .. "|" .. myName .. "|" .. myTier)
                if isOwner and lockdownState.active then
                    publish(sysTopic, "L|" .. myId .. "|" .. myName .. "|1|" .. clean(lockdownState.reason or "Maintenance", 180))
                end
            end
            pcall(poll)
            local wait = POLL_EVERY
            if state.backoff > 0 then wait = state.backoff; state.backoff = 0
            elseif state.failures >= 3 then wait = 20 end
            task.wait(wait)
        end
        if floatGui then floatGui:Destroy() end
        root:Destroy()
    end)
    end)
    if not okChat then warn("[Hyperion] Chat failed to load: " .. tostring(errChat)) end
end)()


pcall(function()
	local tab = Tabs.OldFolder or Tabs.Settings
	if not tab then return end
	tab:AddDropdown("HXLogoMode", {
		Title = "Logo mode",
		Values = { "Normal", "Dynamic Island" },
		Multi = false,
		Default = "Normal",
		Callback = function(v)
			if type(v) == "table" then v = v[1] end
			local mode = (v == "Dynamic Island") and "island" or "normal"
			if _G.__HXApplyLogoMode then
				pcall(_G.__HXApplyLogoMode, mode)
			end
		end,
	})
end)

Window:SelectTab(1)

-- Auto Load Configuration
SaveManager:LoadAutoloadConfig()

pcall(function()
	if type(hxLoadStep) == "function" then hxLoadStep(1.0, "Ready!", 0.15) end
	task.wait(0.1)
	if type(hxLoadFinish) == "function" then hxLoadFinish() end
	task.delay(0.25, function()
		pcall(function()
			if Window and Window.Root then Window.Root.Visible = true end
		end)
	end)
end)


-- Unload The Window
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.5) do
		if Fluent.Unloaded == true or (Fluent.GUI and Fluent.GUI.Parent == nil) then
			if fpsCounterGui then fpsCounterGui:Destroy() end
			openGui:Destroy()

			_G.HyperionHubXIsAlreadyRunning = nil
			task.wait(0.2)

			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "Interface",
				Content = "Confirmed." .. " - " .. "Windows has been unloaded.",
				Duration = 5
			})
		end

		break
	end
end)
